## PDF 81 / 印刷p63

<a id="P081B003"></a><a id="P081B004"></a><a id="P081B005"></a><a id="P081B006"></a><a id="P081B007"></a><a id="P081B008"></a><a id="P081B009"></a><a id="P081B010"></a><a id="P081B011"></a><a id="P081B012"></a><a id="P081B013"></a><a id="P081B014"></a><a id="P081B015"></a><a id="P081B016"></a><a id="P081B017"></a><a id="P081B018"></a><a id="P081B019"></a>
<a id="S0141"></a>
**Source:** PDF81 S0141; native P081B003, P081B004, P081B005, P081B006, P081B007, P081B008, P081B009, P081B010, P081B011, P081B012, P081B013, P081B014, P081B015, P081B016, P081B017, P081B018, P081B019

**Original:** Criterion 1 provided information about the decay of 102Sr into 102Y and then into 102Zr. This provided spectra containing γ ray peaks associated with 102Y and 102Zr, and possibly some counts for γ rays observed in the decay of 102Zr to 102Nb and of 102Nb to 102Mo, and are shown in Figure 6.5a. The aim of this analysis is to conﬁrm the claim made by Hill and Shizuma, that the γ ray intensity pattern observed in 102Zr in the decay path 102Sr to 102Y to 102Zr diﬀers from the pattern observed in the path 102Y to 102Zr. However, the overlapping 150 keV γ ray peak in 102Y and 152 keV peak in 102Zr make it diﬃcult to identify the γ-ray intensities in 102Zr. Therefore, to remove the γ rays associated with 102Y, a 20 ms ion-β time window was used to produce a spectrum containing just 102Y γ rays. A time window of 20 ms was chosen as the β-decay half-life of 102Sr into 102Y is 69 ms, so this short window reduces the probability that 102Y has β decayed into 102Zr, thus providing γ-ray peaks for 102Y only. Figure 6.5b shows the β-gated γ-ray spectrum following the application of this short ion-β time gate. The spectrum presented in Figure 6.5b was then normalised, so that it could be subtracted from Figure 6.5a to leave just the γ rays associated with 102Zr. The 244 keV γ ray peak in 102Y was used to normalise this data because it is the strongest observed transition, the normalised β-gated γ-ray spectrum for the 20 ms ion-β time gate is presented in Figure 6.5c.

**中文:** 条件1提供102Sr衰变到102Y、再到102Zr的信息，因此谱中含102Y和102Zr的γ峰，也可能含少量102Zr→102Nb及102Nb→102Mo的γ计数，见图6.5a。本分析旨在检验Hill和Shizuma所称的两条路径中102Zr的γ强度图样差别。然而102Y的150keV峰与102Zr的152keV峰重叠，难以确定102Zr的γ强度。因此，先使用20ms的离子–β时间窗取得仅包含102Y γ射线的谱，用以去除102Y成分。选择20ms是因为102Sr→102Y的β半衰期为69ms；短窗降低102Y已衰变到102Zr的概率，从而仅留下102Y的γ峰。图6.5b展示施加该短窗后的β门γ谱。随后归一化该谱，并从图6.5a扣除，留下102Zr γ成分。由于244keV是102Y最强的跃迁，采用该峰进行归一；归一后的20ms谱见图6.5c。

<a id="P081B020"></a><a id="P081B021"></a><a id="P081B022"></a><a id="P081B023"></a><a id="P081B024"></a><a id="P081B025"></a><a id="P081B026"></a><a id="P081B027"></a><a id="P081B028"></a><a id="P081B029"></a><a id="P081B030"></a><a id="P081B031"></a>
<a id="S0142"></a>
**Source:** PDF81 S0142; native P081B020, P081B021, P081B022, P081B023, P081B024, P081B025, P081B026, P081B027, P081B028, P081B029, P081B030, P081B031

**Original:** In a separate experiment, conducted by Hill at the TRISTAN facility, which measured the β decay of 102Sr into 102Y, the 150 keV transition in 102Y was measured to be 34% of the intensity of the 244 keV transition [37]. However, in this work, the 150 keV transition in 102Y was found to be 41% of the intensity of the 244 keV transition, which lies within two standard deviations of the previous measurement by Hill [37]. Further analysis of the structure of 102Y is presented in Section 6.4. In Figures 6.5a and 6.5b a clear diﬀerence can be seen in the ratio of the 151:244 keV transitions when the 20 ms ion-β time gate was applied. Furthermore, at γ-ray energies >1 MeV γ-ray peaks associated with 102Zr, at 1059-, 1160-, and 1211 keV, are removed showing that in the 20 ms time window only γ-ray transitions associated with 102Y are observed. The subtraction of Figures 6.5c from 6.5a provided a β-gated γ-ray spectrum for criterion 1 containing just the γ rays associated with 102Zr, and is presented in Figure 6.6b.

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P081B032"></a>
<a id="S0143"></a>
**Source:** PDF81 S0143; native P081B032

![Figure 6.6](assets/figures/figure-6_6.png)

**Original caption:** Figure 6.6: β-gated γ-ray energy spectra using ion-β coincidence time of 1.5 seconds (a) peaks in 102Zr following implantation of 102Sr and subtraction of γ-ray peaks in 102Y (b) peaks in 102Zr following implantation of 102Y in WAS3ABi.

**中文图注:** 离子–β符合时间取1.5s的β门γ能谱：(a)植入102Sr并扣除102Y γ峰后的102Zr峰；(b)WAS3ABi中植入102Y后出现的102Zr峰。

<a id="P083B038"></a>
<a id="T6_1"></a>
### Table 6.1
**Source:** PDF83 P083B038

![Table 6.1](assets/figures/table-6_1.png)

**Original caption:** Table 6.1: The energies and uncertainties listed in the ﬁrst column are from the RIKEN analysis. The remaining four columns show the relative intensities (a) measured at RIKEN for criterion 1, (b) measured at RIKEN for criterion 2, (c) measured at TRISTAN [8] from the low-spin isomeric state in 102Y and (d) deduced by combining TRISTAN and JOSEF studies, as described in [8].

**中文图注:** 第一列能量及误差来自RIKEN分析。后四列相对强度依次为(a)RIKEN条件1，(b)RIKEN条件2，(c)TRISTAN[8]测得的102Y低自旋异能态衰变，(d)按文献[8]结合TRISTAN及JOSEF研究推得的结果。

<a id="P084B002"></a><a id="P084B003"></a><a id="P084B004"></a><a id="P084B005"></a><a id="P084B006"></a><a id="P084B007"></a><a id="P084B008"></a><a id="P084B009"></a><a id="P084B010"></a><a id="P084B011"></a><a id="P084B012"></a><a id="P084B013"></a><a id="P084B014"></a><a id="P084B015"></a><a id="P084B016"></a><a id="P084B017"></a><a id="P084B018"></a><a id="P084B019"></a><a id="P084B020"></a><a id="P084B021"></a><a id="P084B022"></a><a id="P084B023"></a><a id="P084B024"></a><a id="P084B025"></a><a id="P084B026"></a><a id="P084B027"></a><a id="P084B028"></a>
<a id="S0147"></a>
**Source:** PDF84 S0147; native P084B002, P084B003, P084B004, P084B005, P084B006, P084B007, P084B008, P084B009, P084B010, P084B011, P084B012, P084B013, P084B014, P084B015, P084B016, P084B017, P084B018, P084B019, P084B020, P084B021, P084B022, P084B023, P084B024, P084B025, P084B026, P084B027, P084B028

**Original:** Table 6.1 details the relative intensities measured for 102Zr for criterion 1 and criterion 2, compared to the relative intensities found at TRISTAN [8] and JOSEF [9] for the decay of the low-spin and high spin isomeric state in 102Y. The decay of the low-spin isomer in 102Y was investigated at TRISTAN, however, the relative intensities given for the decay of the high-spin isomer in 102Y were deduced by combining the TRISTAN [8] and JOSEF [9] studies. Comparing the relative intensities observed for criterion 1 and 2, there is a stark diﬀerence in the intensity of the γ rays produced. The intensities measured for criterion 1 and criterion 2 have both been normalised to the 152 keV γ ray transition, the same is true for the previous results from TRISTAN and JOSEF. The 160 keV γ ray was seen at RIKEN with a relative intensity of 10% for criterion 2, which lies within one standard deviation of the intensity measured for the decay of the high-spin isomer in 102Y. For criterion 1 the 160 keV transition was not seen at all, which is in-line with the relative intensity of <1% observed at TRISTAN. The 283 keV transition was not observed at TRISTAN or JOSEF, and was only observed in this data with a 3% intensity for criterion 2. The 326 keV transition shows a clear diﬀerence between the relative intensity measured when implanting with 102Sr and 102Y. For criterion 1 the measured relative intensity of 8% lies within one standard deviation of the intensity measured at TRISTAN. Whereas, for criterion 2 the relative intensity of 48% lies within one standard deviation of the intensity measured for the decay of the high-spin isomeric state in 102Y. The 486 keV transition was observed with a relative intensity of 9% when implanting with 102Y, which agrees with the intensity measured for the decay of the high-spin isomeric state of 102Y to within one standard deviation. However, this transition was not seen when implanting with 102Sr, which is in-line with the intensity of <2% measured at TRISTAN. Similarly, the 579 keV transition was not seen when implanting with 102Sr, which is in-line with the intensity of <1% measured at TRISTAN. Whereas, this transition was seen when implanting with 102Y with a relative intensity of 34%, which lies within one standard deviation of the intensity measured for the decay of the high-spin isomeric state of 102Y.

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

The intensity measured when implanting with 102Sr lies within one standard deviation of the intensity measured at TRISTAN, however, this transition was not measured for the decay of the high-spin isomeric state in 102Y. The 763 keV transition was not measured at TRISTAN or JOSEF, and was only

## PDF 85 / 印刷p67

<a id="P085B002"></a><a id="P085B003"></a><a id="P085B004"></a><a id="P085B005"></a><a id="P085B006"></a><a id="P085B007"></a><a id="P085B008"></a><a id="P085B009"></a><a id="P085B010"></a><a id="P085B011"></a><a id="P085B012"></a><a id="P085B013"></a><a id="P085B014"></a><a id="P085B015"></a><a id="P085B016"></a><a id="P085B017"></a><a id="P085B018"></a><a id="P085B019"></a>
<a id="S0149"></a>
**Source:** PDF85 S0149; native P085B002, P085B003, P085B004, P085B005, P085B006, P085B007, P085B008, P085B009, P085B010, P085B011, P085B012, P085B013, P085B014, P085B015, P085B016, P085B017, P085B018, P085B019

**Original:** measured in this data with a relative intensity of 3% for criterion 2. The 1059 keV transition was measured with an intensity of 30% when implanting with 102Sr, which agrees with the intensity measured at TRISTAN to within one standard deviation. When implanting with 102Y the intensity was 13%, which agrees with the intensity measured for the highspin isomeric state in 102Y to within one standard deviation. The 1090 keV transition was not observed when implanting with 102Sr, which is in-line with the intensity of less than 1% measured at TRISTAN. When implanting with 102Y the intensity was measured at 40%, which agrees with the intensity measured for the decay of the high-spin state in 102Y to within one standard deviation. The 1160 keV transition was measured with a relative intensity of 16% when implanting with 102Sr, which agrees with the intensity measured at TRISTAN to within one standard deviation. When implanting with 102Y the 1160 keV transition was measured with a relative intensity of 5%, but this transition was not measured for the decay of the high-spin state in 102Y in previous work. Finally, the 1211 keV transition was measured with a relative intensity of 37% when implanting with 102Sr, which agrees with the intensity measured at TRISTAN to within one standard deviation. Whereas, when implanting with 102Y the 1211 keV transition was measured with a relative intensity of 8%, which agrees with the intensity measured for the decay of the high-spin state in 102Y to within two standard deviations.

**Original:** From the β-gated γ-ray singles spectra it is clear that the γ-ray intensity pattern observed when gating on 102Sr implantations in WAS3ABi (criterion 1) diﬀers from the γ-ray pattern observed when gating on 102Y implantations in WAS3ABi (criterion 2). This observation supports the claim previously made by Hill [8] and Shizuma [9], and provides further evidence for the existence of a second β-decaying isomeric state in 102Y.

**中文:** β门γ单谱清楚表明，在WAS3ABi中选102Sr植入（条件1）所见的γ强度图样，与选102Y植入（条件2）所见图样不同。该观测支持Hill[8]和Shizuma[9]此前的主张，并为102Y存在第二个β衰变异能态提供了进一步证据。

<a id="P086B003"></a><a id="P086B004"></a><a id="P086B005"></a><a id="P086B006"></a><a id="P086B007"></a><a id="P086B008"></a><a id="P086B009"></a><a id="P086B010"></a><a id="P086B011"></a>
<a id="S0152"></a>
**Source:** PDF86 S0152; native P086B003, P086B004, P086B005, P086B006, P086B007, P086B008, P086B009, P086B010, P086B011

**Original:** The time between two γ ray emissions was measured using the EURICA array, as described in Section 5.2. The γ-γ coincidence time distribution for criterion 1 and 2 is shown in Figure 6.7. In this ﬁgure, (a) shows the time distribution for criterion 1, and (b) shows the time distribution for criterion 2. Using this information a coincidence time window of ±300 ns was used to create a γ-γ matrix. True γ-γ coincidences should occur in the nanosecond range, therefore, by limiting the accepted coincidence time to 300 ns, background contamination is reduced. In contrast to the ion-β coincidence time gate, the number of random coincidences that lie within the 300 ns time window were too low to subtract and provide any clear beneﬁt.

**中文:** 两个γ发射之间的时间差由EURICA阵列测量，见5.2节。图6.7给出条件1、2的γ–γ符合时间分布，分别为(a)、(b)。由此选择±300ns的符合窗口构建γ–γ矩阵。真正的γ–γ符合应发生在纳秒尺度，因此把接受时间限制到300ns可降低背景。与离子–β时间门不同，300ns内的随机符合太少，扣除不能带来明显益处。

<a id="P086B028"></a><a id="P086B029"></a><a id="P086B030"></a><a id="P086B031"></a>
<a id="S0153"></a>
**Source:** PDF86 S0153; native P086B028, P086B029, P086B030, P086B031

**Original:** Following the application of a γ-γ coincidence time gate of <300 ns a symmetrical γ-γ matrix was produced. From this matrix coincidence gates were applied to each of the γ-ray peaks listed in Table 6.1. Observed coincidences are presented in Table 6.2 for both criterion 1 and 2, and compared to the coincidences observed at TRISTAN [8] and

**中文:** 施加<300ns的γ–γ符合时间门后，得到对称γ–γ矩阵。再对表6.1所列每个γ峰施加符合门。表6.2列出条件1、2所见的符合，并与TRISTAN[8]及JOSEF[9]所见符合比较。

<a id="P087B008"></a><a id="P087B009"></a><a id="P087B010"></a><a id="P087B011"></a><a id="P087B012"></a><a id="P087B013"></a>
<a id="S0155"></a>
**Source:** PDF87 S0155; native P087B008, P087B009, P087B010, P087B011, P087B012, P087B013

**Original:** In this work, gating on the 152 keV transition in the γ-γ matrix created for criterion 1 showed coincidences with the 326-, 743-, and 1059 keV transitions, which agrees with the coincidences observed at TRISTAN. The same gate made for criterion 2, however, identiﬁed coincidences with the 160-, 326-, 486, 579-, 743, 763, 1059-, and 1090 keV transitions, which agrees with the coincidences observed at JOSEF. A comparison between the 152 keV coincidence gates for criterion 1 and 2 is illustrated in Figure 6.8.

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P087B014"></a><a id="P087B015"></a><a id="P087B016"></a><a id="P087B017"></a><a id="P087B018"></a><a id="P087B019"></a><a id="P087B020"></a><a id="P087B021"></a><a id="P087B022"></a><a id="P087B023"></a><a id="P087B024"></a><a id="P087B025"></a><a id="P087B026"></a>
<a id="S0156"></a>
**Source:** PDF87 S0156; native P087B014, P087B015, P087B016, P087B017, P087B018, P087B019, P087B020, P087B021, P087B022, P087B023, P087B024, P087B025, P087B026

**Original:** As shown in Table 6.1, the 160 keV transition was not observed for criterion 1. However, for criterion 2, the 160 keV transition was coincident with the 152-, 326-, 579-, and 1090 keV transitions. At JOSEF only the 152-, and 579 keV transitions were observed in coincidence spectra, due to comparatively low statistics obtained in previous work. The 283 keV transition identiﬁed in Table 6.1 was only observed for criterion 2 with a relative intensity of 3%, and was therefore too weak to identify coincidences. The 326 keV transition was only coincident with the 152 keV transition for criterion 1, which agrees with the TRISTAN measurements. Whereas, for criterion 2 the 326 keV transition was found to be coincident with the 152-, 160-, 486-, 579-, 764-, and 1059 keV transitions. At JOSEF only the 152-, and 486 keV transitions were found to be coincident with the 326 keV transition. At JOSEF, the 160- and 1059 keV transitions were likely not seen in this coincidence gate due to low statistics. As shown in Table 6.1 the 764 keV transition was observed in the previous work at TRISTAN or JOSEF. A comparison between the 326 keV coincidence

<a id="P089B002"></a><a id="P089B003"></a><a id="P089B004"></a><a id="P089B005"></a><a id="P089B006"></a><a id="P089B007"></a><a id="P089B008"></a><a id="P089B009"></a><a id="P089B010"></a><a id="P089B011"></a><a id="P089B012"></a><a id="P089B013"></a><a id="P089B014"></a><a id="P089B015"></a><a id="P089B016"></a><a id="P089B017"></a><a id="P089B018"></a>

**Source:** PDF89 S0158; native P089B002, P089B003, P089B004, P089B005, P089B006, P089B007, P089B008, P089B009, P089B010, P089B011, P089B012, P089B013, P089B014, P089B015, P089B016, P089B017, P089B018

**Original:** As presented in Table 6.1 both the 486- and 579 keV transitions were not observed for criterion 1. However, for criterion 2, the 486 keV transition was coincident with the 152-, and 326 keV transitions, which agrees with the results obtained at JOSEF. Whereas, the 579 keV transition was coincident with the 152-, 160-, 326-, 764-, and 1090 keV transitions. However, at JOSEF only the 152-, and 160 keV coincidences were found to be coincident with the 579 keV transition due to low statistics. The 743 keV transition was only found to be coincident with the 152 keV transition in criterion 1, which agrees with the previous work at TRISTAN. However, for criterion 2, the 743 keV transition was only observed with a relative intensity of 4% and was therefore too weak to identify coincidences. In Table 6.1 it was shown that the 764 keV transtion was only observed for criterion 2 with a relative intensity of 3%, and not seen for criterion 1 or in previous work at TRISTAN and JOSEF. For criterion 2 the 764 keV transition was found to be coincident with the 152-, 326-, and 579 keV transitions. The 1059 keV transition was only coincident with the 152 keV transition in criterion 1 in this work, which agrees with the previous work at TRISTAN. For criterion 2 the 1059 keV transition was coincident with the 152-, and 326 keV transitions, whereas at JOSEF only the 152 keV coincidence was observed, possibly due to low statistics. A comparison between the 1059 keV coincidence gates for criterion

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

**Original:** Finally, Table 6.1 shows that the 1090 keV transition was not observed for criterion 1. However, for criterion 2 the 1090 keV transition was found to be coincident with the 152-, 160-, and 579 keV transitions, which agrees with the results obtained at JOSEF. In this work, for both criterion 1 and 2, and also in the previous studies at TRISTAN and JOSEF no coincidences were observed for the 1160- and 1211 keV transitions, even though for criterion 1 both transitions were observed with a high relative intensity of 16% and 37%, respectively. It is concluded that both of these transitions directly feed the ground state in 102Zr, and any transitions feeding 1160- and 1211 keV transitions were too weak to observe in this work. Further to the work of Hill and Shizuma, an additional 764 keV γ-ray transition was observed in this work, coincidence spectra for the 579- and 764 keV transitions are shown in Figure 6.11. This 764 keV transition is discussed in more detail

The 1037 keV transition was measured with a relative intensity of 15%, which lies within one standard deviation of the intensity measured at TRISTAN. The 1105 keV transition was measured in this work to have a relative intensity of 21%, which lies within one standard deviations of the intensity measured at TRISTAN. The 1193 keV transition was measured to have a relative intensity of 23%, which also lies within three standard deviations of the intensity measured at TRISTAN. **Original:** A number of transitions identiﬁed in the experiment at TRISTAN were not observed in this work due to reduced statistics in this work compared to TRISTAN. At TRISTAN the 36-, 103-, 128-, 312-, 404-, 437-, 498-, 655-, 814-, 850-, 1446-, and 1689 keV transitions were all observed with a relative intensity of <7%, and were not observed in this work. However, the relative intensities measured for the transitions observed in this work are in good agreement with the intensities measured at the TRISTAN facility [37].

**Original:** In general, there is good agreement between the coincidences observed in this work and at TRISTAN. The 68 keV transition was coincident with the 150-, 186-, 244-, 1037-, and 1378 keV transitions. However, in this work, a coincidence was not observed with the 94 keV transition, which was observed at TRISTAN. The 94 keV transition was not observed due to low statistics. Figure 6.12 shows the 68 keV gate for this experiment, whereas Figure 6.13 shows the same gate in the TRISTAN experiment, taken from [37]. Comparing Figures 6.12 and 6.13 the height of the coincidence peaks clearly diﬀer. At TRISTAN the 244 keV transition was the highest peak observed, whereas in this work the 150 keV transition was the strongest, and the 186-, and 244 keV transitions were measured with roughly the same peak height. These diﬀerence are due to the detector eﬃciency, the detectors used during the TRISTAN experiment appear to have an eﬃciency curve which peaks slightly higher than the detectors used at RIKEN.

**Original:** observed in this work due to low statistics. The 186 keV transition was only observed with a relative intensity of 11% in the singles spectra, and only a tentative coincidence with the 68 keV transition was identiﬁed in this work. At TRISTAN coincidences with the 244 keV transition, as well as tentative coincidences with the 94-, 150-, 218-, and 1193 keV transitions, were observed, which were not seen in this work due to low statistics. The 209 keV transition was observed with a relative intensity of 15% in the singles spectra, but was too weak to gate on in this work. Coincidences with the 254-, and 437 keV transitions, as well as tentative coincidences with the 103 keV transition, were observed at TRISTAN but were not identiﬁed in this work due to low statistics. Similarly, the 218 keV transition was observed with a relative intensity of 10% in the singles spectra, but was too weak to gate on in this work. At TRISTAN coincidences with the 94 keV transition, as well as tentative coincidences with the 186 keV transition were observed, but low statistics meant they were too weak to observe in this work. The 244 keV transition was the strongest transition observed in this work, and was observed in coincidence with the 68-, 186-, 254-, 1037-, 1105-, and 1193 keV transitions. Figure 6.14a shows the coincidence gate for the 244 keV transition. This agrees with the transitions observed at TRISTAN, however, additional tentative coincidences with the 655-, 850-, 1378-, and 1445 keV transitions were not observed due to low statistics. The 186 keV coincidence was not previously observed at TRISTAN, however, a coincidence with the 244 keV transition was observed at TRISTAN

<a id="P095B022"></a><a id="P095B023"></a><a id="P095B024"></a><a id="P095B025"></a><a id="P095B026"></a><a id="P095B027"></a><a id="P095B028"></a><a id="P095B029"></a><a id="P095B030"></a><a id="P095B031"></a><a id="P095B032"></a><a id="P095B033"></a>

**Original:** The 254 keV transition was observed in coincidence with the 150-, 244-, and 1193 keV transitions, which agrees with the coincidences observed at TRISTAN. However, at TRIS- TAN an additional coincidence was observed with the 94 keV transition, which was not identiﬁed in this work due to low statistics. The 1037 keV transition was observed in coincidence with the 68-, and 244 keV transitions in this work. However, at TRISTAN a coincidence was only observed with the 68 keV transition. The 1037 keV gate obtained for this work is presented in Figure 6.14b. The 1105 keV transition was observed in coincidence with the 94-, 150-, and 244 keV transitions, which agrees with the coincidences observed at TRISTAN. Finally, the 1193 keV transition was observed in coincidence with the 94-, 150-, 186-, 244-, and 254 keV transitions, which agrees with the coincidences observed at TRISTAN. However, at TRISTAN the 150-, and 244 keV transitions were only assigned as tentative coincidences. The data collected in this work has a very low level of background,

Since applying BCS theory to atomic nuclei, pairing correlations have played a major role in resolving an outstanding problem of nuclear physics, namely the energy

**Original:** These pairing force strengths, taken from [69], were calculated for N=63, A∼100 nuclei. In these deﬁnitions it can be seen that Gp is only dependent on the mass number. ## PDF 101 / 印刷p83

<a id="P101B003"></a><a id="P101B004"></a><a id="P101B005"></a><a id="P101B006"></a><a id="P101B007"></a><a id="P101B008"></a><a id="P101B009"></a><a id="P101B010"></a><a id="P101B011"></a><a id="P101B012"></a><a id="P101B013"></a><a id="P101B014"></a><a id="P101B015"></a><a id="P101B016"></a>
<a id="S0192"></a>
**Source:** PDF101 S0192; native P101B003, P101B004, P101B005, P101B006, P101B007, P101B008, P101B009, P101B010, P101B011, P101B012, P101B013, P101B014, P101B015, P101B016

**Original:** To visualise how the single-particle states vary with deformation a Nilsson diagram for protons in the range 28 < Z < 50 and a Nilsson diagram for neutrons in the range 50 < N < 82 was produced. To achieve this input parameters for ϵ2 and ϵ4; the number of protons and neutrons; the nucleon core; and the pairing force for protons and neutrons (Equations 7.5a and 7.5b) were provided. Additionally, the optimisation of κ and µ was investigated. In Equations 7.8a and 7.8b C is related only to κ, whereas D is related to both κ and µ. Table 7.1 shows relationships between ∆κ, the spin-orbit term (l.s) and ∆E, as well as relationships between µ and ∆E. From this table it can be seen that the relationships vary depending on the angular momentum, l. The equations listed in Table 7.1 provide a way of predicting the change in energy of a state of a certain l-value as a result of changing κ or µ. Each of these equations was obtained by making changes independently to the κ and µ values read by the BCS code [68] and observing the eﬀect on the energy of the orbitals. It was important to ensure both κ and µ were optimised for the A∼100 region to ensure the orbitals were correctly ordered in the Nilsson diagram.

## PDF 104 / 印刷p86

<a id="P104B002"></a><a id="P104B003"></a><a id="P104B004"></a><a id="P104B005"></a><a id="P104B006"></a><a id="P104B007"></a><a id="P104B008"></a><a id="P104B009"></a><a id="P104B010"></a><a id="P104B011"></a><a id="P104B012"></a><a id="P104B013"></a><a id="P104B014"></a><a id="P104B015"></a><a id="P104B016"></a><a id="P104B017"></a><a id="P104B018"></a><a id="P104B019"></a><a id="P104B020"></a><a id="P104B021"></a>
<a id="S0193"></a>
**Source:** PDF104 S0193; native P104B002, P104B003, P104B004, P104B005, P104B006, P104B007, P104B008, P104B009, P104B010, P104B011, P104B012, P104B013, P104B014, P104B015, P104B016, P104B017, P104B018, P104B019, P104B020, P104B021

**Original:** In Figures 7.2 and 7.3 the red line, labelled with λ indicates the energy of the Fermi surface as a function of deformation. The Nilsson orbitals are labelled as Kπ [N nz Λ]. N is the principal quantum number of the major shell, nz deﬁnes the number of nodes in the wave function in the z-direction, and Λ is the component of the orbital angular momentum along the z-axis. The Nilsson diagrams presented in Figures 7.2 and 7.3 show only prolate deformation, as previous work suggests 102Y is more likely to have prolate deformation [70] than oblate deformation, or to be spherical. In the work by J. Skalski, et al. [70], the shapes of nuclei in the region Z ∼40, N > 56 were calculated using the Nilsson-Strutinsky method with the cranked Woods-Saxon average potential and a monopole pairing residual interaction. These calculations looked at the oblate-prolate energy diﬀerence, deﬁned as ∆Eop = Eo −Ep, where Eo is the lowest energy for negative β2 and Ep is the lowest energy with zero or positive β2, which is related to ϵ2 by Equation 7.11. Negative values of ∆Eop indicate an oblate deformation (ϵ2 < 0), whereas positive values of ∆Eop indicate a prolate deformation (ϵ2 > 0). In the work by J. Skalski [70], et al., calculations were only performed for even-even nuclei. Therefore, to predict the deformation of 102Y, an odd-odd nucleus (N=63, Z=39), a comparison was made between neighbouring even-even nuclei, namely 100Sr (N=62, Z=38), 102Sr (N=64, Z=38), 102Zr (N=62, Z=40), and 104Zr (N=64, Z=40). All neighbouring even-even nuclei were predicted by J. Skalski [70], et al., to have prolate deformation, with β2 = 0.35, 0.36, 0.36 and 0.37 respectively, leading to the conclusion that 102Y is likely to also have a prolate deformation.

**中文:** 图7.2和7.3中标为λ的红线表示Fermi能随形变的变化。Nilsson轨道记为Kπ[N nz Λ]；N为主壳量子数，nz为波函数沿z方向的节点数，Λ为轨道角动量在z轴上的分量。两图只给prolate形变，因为此前工作[70]提示102Y更可能是prolate，而非oblate或球形。Skalski等[70]用Nilsson–Strutinsky方法、推转Woods–Saxon平均势和单极配对残余相互作用，计算Z∼40、N>56核的形状。其oblate–prolate能差定义为ΔEop=Eo−Ep，Eo为负β2最低能量，Ep为零或正β2最低能量；β2与ε2的关系见式7.11。负ΔEop表示oblate，正值表示prolate。该研究只计算偶偶核，因此对odd–odd的102Y（N=63、Z=39），本工作比较相邻偶偶100Sr、102Sr、102Zr及104Zr。这些邻核均被Skalski等预测为prolate，β2依次为0.35、0.36、0.36、0.37，因此推断102Y也可能呈prolate形变。

<a id="P104B022"></a><a id="P104B023"></a><a id="P104B024"></a><a id="P104B025"></a><a id="P104B026"></a><a id="P104B027"></a><a id="P104B028"></a><a id="P104B029"></a>
<a id="S0194"></a>
**Source:** PDF104 S0194; native P104B022, P104B023, P104B024, P104B025, P104B026, P104B027, P104B028, P104B029

**Original:** On the prolate side of the Nilsson diagram the lowest-lying orbit from each spherical single-particle orbit has the highest nz value, where the maximum value of nz is N. This can be seen in the Nilsson diagram presented in Figure 2.7, in Chapter 2. Furthermore, the sum of nz and Λ must be even if N is even, indicating even parity, and their sum must be odd if N is odd, indicating odd parity. In the Nilsson diagrams presented in Figures 7.2 and 7.3 an even parity orbital is indicated using a solid line, whereas an odd parity orbital is indicated using a dashed line. On the y-axis of the Nilsson diagram, the single particle energies are presented in units of ¯hω, calculated using [30]:

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

## PDF 105 / 印刷p87

<a id="P105B002"></a><a id="P105B003"></a><a id="P105B004"></a><a id="P105B005"></a><a id="P105B006"></a><a id="P105B007"></a><a id="P105B008"></a><a id="P105B009"></a><a id="P105B010"></a><a id="P105B011"></a><a id="P105B012"></a>
<a id="S0195"></a>
**Source:** PDF105 S0195; native P105B002, P105B003, P105B004, P105B005, P105B006, P105B007, P105B008, P105B009, P105B010, P105B011, P105B012

**Original:** To produce the Nilsson diagrams presented in Figures 7.2 and 7.3 values for κ and µ were obtained from previous calculations by T. Bengtsson and I. Ragnarsson [71]. These calculations were performed within the cranked Nilsson-Strutinsky framework to predict the high-spin states of nuclei. In the work by T. Bengtsson and I. Ragnarsson the values of κ and µ used in calculations for various values of N, the principal quantum number, were stated. For this work N=4 was most appropriate, considering the position of the Fermi surface. For N=4, T. Bengtsson and I. Ragnarsson used the values κ = 0.065 and µ = 0.57, for protons, and κ = 0.07 and µ = 0.39 for neutrons. However, the BCS calculations performed in this work did not converge when using µ = 0.57 for protons, and this parameter was lowered to µ = 0.32. This resulted in a downward shift of orbitals by ≈0.05 ¯hω, which does not aﬀect the ordering of the levels in the Nilsson diagram.

**中文:** 图7.2和7.3采用Bengtsson及Ragnarsson[71]此前计算的κ、μ值。那些计算在推转Nilsson–Strutinsky框架下预测高自旋态，并给出不同主量子数N所用的参数。考虑Fermi能位置，本工作采用N=4最合适。文献对N=4的质子取κ=0.065、μ=0.57，中子取κ=0.07、μ=0.39。然而本工作在质子μ=0.57时BCS不收敛，因此将其降至0.32。这使轨道整体约下降0.05ħω，但未改变Nilsson图的能级排序。

<a id="P105B014"></a><a id="P105B015"></a><a id="P105B016"></a><a id="P105B017"></a><a id="P105B018"></a><a id="P105B019"></a>
<a id="S0196"></a>
**Source:** PDF105 S0196; native P105B014, P105B015, P105B016, P105B017, P105B018, P105B019

**Original:** From the single particle energies, quasiparticle energies were calculated in the BCS code using Equation 7.6. However, some of these quasiparticle states may already be occupied. According to Pauli’s exclusion principle, which states that two or more identical fermions cannot occupy the same state simultaneously [72], these occupied states need to be blocked from further calculations. The inclusion of blocking is highly important in reproducing the correct energy spectrum. This blocking eﬀect leads to a reduction in the pair gap, and so

**中文:** BCS代码由单粒子能按式7.6计算准粒子能。然而某些准粒子态可能已被占据；依据Pauli不相容原理，相同费米子不能同时占据同一状态[72]，因此后续计算须阻塞这些已占据态。阻塞对重现正确能谱很重要，会降低配对能隙，于是式7.2改为以下形式[67,p238]：

<a id="P105B025"></a><a id="P105B026"></a><a id="P105B027"></a><a id="P105B028"></a><a id="P105B029"></a><a id="P105B030"></a><a id="P105B031"></a>
<a id="S0197"></a>
**Source:** PDF105 S0197; native P105B025, P105B026, P105B027, P105B028, P105B029, P105B030, P105B031

**Original:** The implementation of blocking and the correct assignment of κ and µ provide the correct ordering of states. However, in order to obtain more reliable assignments for the energy of the states a further correction to the quasiparticle energies was required. In order to identify this correction factor for 102Y, initial calculations were performed for the odd-Z nucleus 101Y [73], and odd-N nuclei 101Sr [74] and 103Zr [75]. 103Y was not included in these calculations as very little is currently known about the structure of this nucleus. For this initial calculation the input parameters ϵ2 = 0.3 [70], κ = 0.065 and µ = 0.32, for

**中文:** 加入阻塞并恰当选择κ、μ可得到正确能级排序，但要获得更可靠的态能量，还需要修正准粒子能。为确定102Y的修正，本工作先计算odd-Z核101Y[73]及odd-N核101Sr[74]和103Zr[75]。由于103Y结构所知很少，没有把它纳入。初次计算取ε2=0.3[70]，质子κ=0.065、μ=0.32，

**Original:** protons, and κ = 0.07 and µ = 0.39 for neutrons [71] were used. The energy calculated using these parameters for the low-lying excited states in 101Y, 101Sr, and 103Zr is presented in Table 7.2, under 1st calculation, and compared with literature values for each of these

To improve the agreement between the calculated energies and the measured values modiﬁcations were made to the quasiparticle energies, as shown in Table 7.3. In Table 7.2 the column labelled 2nd calculation shows the energies calculated using the blocked BCS calculations after implementing the changes to the quasiparticle energies, ϵ2, κ, and µ were kept the same as in the 1st calculation.

## PDF 108 / 印刷p90

<a id="P108B002"></a><a id="P108B003"></a><a id="P108B004"></a><a id="P108B005"></a><a id="P108B006"></a><a id="P108B007"></a><a id="P108B008"></a><a id="P108B009"></a><a id="P108B010"></a><a id="P108B011"></a><a id="P108B012"></a><a id="P108B013"></a><a id="P108B014"></a><a id="P108B015"></a><a id="P108B016"></a><a id="P108B017"></a><a id="P108B018"></a><a id="P108B019"></a><a id="P108B020"></a><a id="P108B021"></a>
<a id="S0203"></a>
**Source:** PDF108 S0203; native P108B002, P108B003, P108B004, P108B005, P108B006, P108B007, P108B008, P108B009, P108B010, P108B011, P108B012, P108B013, P108B014, P108B015, P108B016, P108B017, P108B018, P108B019, P108B020, P108B021

**Original:** second excited state is a coupling of a 1/2[411] neutron and a 5/2[422] proton. This is the same proton state as was calculated for the ground and ﬁrst excited state of 102Y, and the neutron state is the second excited state of the odd-N neighbour 101Sr. The fourth excited state for 102Y is a coupling of a 5/2[413] neutron and a 5/2[422] proton. As before this is the same proton state as calculated for the ground, ﬁrst, and second excited states, but the neutron state was not calculated or found in the literature for odd-N neighbours. However, in Figure 7.3, the 5/2[413] state is found between the 5/2[532] and 1/2[411] states for ϵ2 = 0.3, so it is expected to have an appropriate energy to contribute to a low-lying state in 102Y. The ﬁfth excited state in 102Y is a coupling of a 5/2[532] neutron and a 3/2[301] proton. This is expected as the ﬁrst excited state in the odd-Z neighbour 101Y is 3/2[301], the ground state in 101Y has exhausted pairings with neutron states close to the Fermi surface. The proton state is paired with the ground state of the odd-N neighbours 101Sr and 103Zr. Finally, the sixth excited state calculated for 102Y is a coupling of a 5/2[532] neutron and a 5/2[303] proton. The neutron state is the same as calculated for the ﬁfth excited state of 102Y, and the proton state is the second excited state for the odd-Z neighbour 101Y. The neutron and proton state coupling calculated for 102Y produce the possible states listed in the ﬁrst column of Table 7.4. These possible states come from the fact that the last odd neutron and the last odd proton can couple with their angular momentum parallel or anti-parallel. The Gallagher-Moszkowski rules [76] were used to calculate which of these possible states was the lowest energy state in 102Y.

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P108B023"></a><a id="P108B024"></a><a id="P108B025"></a><a id="P108B026"></a><a id="P108B027"></a>
<a id="S0205"></a>
**Source:** PDF108 S0205; native P108B023, P108B024, P108B025, P108B026, P108B027

**Original:** The Gallagher-Moszkowski coupling rules, presented in Equation 7.13 [76] provide conditions for coupling the last odd proton and last odd neutron to provide the lowest energy conﬁguration. The coupling of the last odd proton and last odd neutron are known as residual nucleon-nucleon interactions, and are not included in the blocked BCS calculations. However, residual interactions can result in a diﬀerence of a few hundred keV in

**中文:** 式7.13所示Gallagher–Moszkowski耦合规则[76]给出奇质子与奇中子耦合形成最低能组态的条件。最后奇质子与奇中子的耦合称为残余核子–核子相互作用，blocked BCS计算未包含它。不过，残余相互作用可使激发能相差几百keV[26,p60]。

## PDF 109 / 印刷p91

<a id="P109B008"></a><a id="P109B009"></a><a id="P109B010"></a><a id="P109B011"></a><a id="P109B012"></a><a id="P109B013"></a><a id="P109B014"></a><a id="P109B015"></a><a id="P109B016"></a>
<a id="S0206"></a>
**Source:** PDF109 S0206; native P109B008, P109B009, P109B010, P109B011, P109B012, P109B013, P109B014, P109B015, P109B016

**Original:** It can be seen in Equation 7.13 that lower energy states are produced when particles with the same intrinsic spin projections couple their angular momenta parallel, and when particles with opposite intrinsic spin projections couple their angular momenta antiparallel [76]. Consider the ground state calculated for 102Y, from Table 7.4, which is a mixture of a 5/2[532] neutron state and 5/2[422] proton state. There are two ways in which these proton and neutron states can couple. If the proton and neutron states couple with aligned angular momentum this produces a 5−state, if the states couple with anti-aligned angular momentum this produces a 0−state. The proton and neutron states are both 5/2, and both have Λ = 2, and therefore both satisfy the condition Ω= Λ + 1

**中文:** 式7.13表明，两个粒子的内禀自旋投影相同，其角动量平行耦合时较低；内禀自旋投影相反，其角动量反平行耦合时较低[76]。表7.4中102Y计算基态由5/2[532]中子态与5/2[422]质子态组成。两者可有两种耦合：角动量同向产生5−，反向产生0−。两个轨道均为5/2且Λ=2，因此均满足式7.13a的Ω=Λ+1/2条件。

<a id="P109B018"></a><a id="P109B019"></a><a id="P109B020"></a>
<a id="S0207"></a>
**Source:** PDF109 S0207; native P109B018, P109B019, P109B020

**Original:** lowest energy conﬁguration for the ground state of 102Y is therefore with aligned angular momentum, to produce a 5−ground state. The states with the lowest energy found using the Gallagher-Moszkowski coupling rules are presented in the ﬁnal column of Table 7.4.

**中文:** 所以102Y的最低能基态组态为角动量同向排列，产生5−基态。根据Gallagher–Moszkowski规则确定的各最低能态列于表7.4最后一列。

<a id="P109B021"></a>
<a id="S0208"></a>
**Source:** PDF109 S0208; native P109B021

**Original:** Considering the electromagnetic transition selection rules [20, p.334],

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P109B026"></a><a id="P109B027"></a><a id="P109B028"></a><a id="P109B029"></a><a id="P109B030"></a><a id="P109B031"></a><a id="P109B032"></a>
<a id="S0209"></a>
**Source:** PDF109 S0209; native P109B026, P109B027, P109B028, P109B029, P109B030, P109B031, P109B032

**Original:** therefore, if there is no change in parity (π) then even electric transitions are possible (e.g. E2, E4) and odd magnetic transitions are possible (e.g. M1, M3). However, if there is a change in parity then odd electric transitions are possible (e.g. E1, E3) and even magnetic transitions are possible (e.g. M2, M4). Ii and If are the angular momentum of the initial and ﬁnal states respectively and L is the multipolarity of the transition. The two possible ground state conﬁgurations for 102Y, shown in Table 7.4, are 5−and 0−. The lower energy state of this pair is the 5−state, and according to the ﬁnal column in Table 7.4 all of the

<a id="P111B004"></a><a id="P111B005"></a><a id="P111B006"></a><a id="P111B007"></a><a id="P111B008"></a>
<a id="S0215"></a>
**Source:** PDF111 S0215; native P111B004, P111B005, P111B006, P111B007, P111B008

**Original:** The 0−state is the higher energy state of the 5−, 0−pair, and was assigned an excitation energy of 300 keV, because typically the coupling of the last odd nucleons results in an energy diﬀerence of a few hundred keV [26, p. 60], which places the 0−state between the 2+ and the 4+ state. The allowed transitions for the γ decay of higher-lying excited states to the 0−state, and from the 0−state to lower-lying states are presented in Table 7.7.

**中文:** 0−是5−、0−这一对中较高的态。本工作给它指定300keV激发能，因为最后两个奇核子的耦合通常造成几百keV能差[26,p60]；这使0−位于2+和4+态之间。表7.7列出较高激发态向0−、以及0−向较低态的γ衰变所允许的跃迁。

<a id="P111B009"></a>
<a id="S0216"></a>
**Source:** PDF111 S0216; native P111B009

**Original:** γ ray Energy (keV) Ii If Allowed transitions Weisskopf estimate (s−1)

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P111B017"></a><a id="P111B018"></a><a id="P111B019"></a><a id="P111B020"></a><a id="P111B021"></a><a id="P111B022"></a><a id="P111B023"></a>
<a id="S0217"></a>
**Source:** PDF111 S0217; native P111B017, P111B018, P111B019, P111B020, P111B021, P111B022, P111B023

**Original:** A comparison between Tables 7.7 and 7.5 clearly shows that transitions to, and from, the 0−state are much less likely than transitions to the 5−state in 102Y. In Tables 7.7 transitions from the ﬁrst and second 0+ states to the 0−state are forbidden to decay by γ emission, as L = 0 in Equation 7.14. In both of these cases internal conversion becomes a more likely decay path. Instead of producing a γ-ray photon, in the process of internal conversion the electromagnetic ﬁeld interacts with atomic electrons causing one of the electrons to be emitted from the atom.

**中文:** 比较表7.7与7.5可见，涉及0−的跃迁远比向102Y 5−基态的跃迁不易发生。表7.7中第一个和第二个0+态到0−态因式7.14给L=0，不能通过γ发射衰变。在这两种情形下，内转换成为更可能的衰变方式。内转换不产生γ光子，而是核电磁场与原子电子相互作用，将一个电子从原子中发射出来。

## PDF 112 / 印刷p94

<a id="P112B005"></a><a id="P112B006"></a><a id="P112B007"></a><a id="P112B008"></a><a id="P112B009"></a><a id="P112B010"></a><a id="P112B011"></a><a id="P112B012"></a><a id="P112B013"></a>
<a id="S0218"></a>
**Source:** PDF112 S0218; native P112B005, P112B006, P112B007, P112B008, P112B009, P112B010, P112B011, P112B012, P112B013

**Original:** Chapter 4 detailed the analysis conducted on data from the IGISOL facility, at the University of Jyv¨askyl¨a, which consisted of two experiments. The ﬁrst experiment utilised the Ramsey cleaning technique to separate the ground and isomeric state in 102Y. As described in Section 3.4, the two isomeric states in 102Y were not cleanly separated. Figure 4.3, in Chapter 4, shows a comparison between the intensity ratios of six transitions in 102Y relative to the 152 keV transition. When taking an average of these intensity ratios at each frequency step there is a general agreement with the intensity ratios measured for the high-spin state in 102Y in previous work by Hill and Shizuma. These results suggested the high-spin state was primarily produced in the ﬁrst IGISOL experiment.

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P112B014"></a><a id="P112B015"></a><a id="P112B016"></a><a id="P112B017"></a><a id="P112B018"></a><a id="P112B019"></a><a id="P112B020"></a><a id="P112B021"></a><a id="P112B022"></a><a id="P112B023"></a><a id="P112B024"></a><a id="P112B025"></a><a id="P112B026"></a>
<a id="S0219"></a>
**Source:** PDF112 S0219; native P112B014, P112B015, P112B016, P112B017, P112B018, P112B019, P112B020, P112B021, P112B022, P112B023, P112B024, P112B025, P112B026

**Original:** The second experiment measured the decay of 102Y into 102Zr without implementing the Ramsey cleaning technique. The aim of this experiment was to conﬁrm the transitions previously observed for the decay of 102Y into 102Zr by Hill and Shizuma, and to provide comparison with the published intensity ratios. In the second experiment at IGISOL γ ray intensities and γ-γ coincidences were identiﬁed. The γ-ray intensities measured for 102Zr in this work indicated a ∼22% contribution from the low-spin state in 102Y measured at TRISTAN [8] and a ∼78% contribution from the high-spin state measured at JOSEF [9]. From this experiment it is concluded that both the ground and isomeric states were produced at IGISOL, and further work is required to achieve the separation of these two states in 102Y for the independent measurement of their subsequent decay. Using the γ-γ coincidence information presented in Table 4.2 a level scheme was produced and compared with previous work by Hill [8] and Shizuma [9]. The ﬁnal level scheme produced in this work is presented in Figure 8.1. The width of the arrows were determined from the

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

**Original:** Comparing Figures 8.1 and 8.2 there are a number of similarities. In both ﬁgures the 326 keV transition depopulates the ﬁrst 4+ state in 102Zr, feeding the 2+ →0+ 152 keV transition to the ground state. In Figure 8.2a the 743 keV transition depopulates the tentatively assigned second 0+ state, feeding the ﬁrst 2+ state, which agrees with the level scheme produced in this work. The 1211 keV transition in Figures 8.2a and b depopulates the tentatively assigned second 2+ state and directly feeds the ground state in 102Zr. This agrees with the placement of the 1211 keV transition in the level scheme produced in this work. The 1159 keV transition shown in Figure 8.2a also directly feeds the ground state, which agrees with the level scheme produced in this work. In Figure 8.2b, the 486 keV transition (incorrectly labelled as a 468 keV transition in the level scheme) depopulates the ﬁrst 6+ state in 102Zr, feeding the ﬁrst 4+ state. This placement agrees with the level scheme produced in this work. The 159 keV transition in Figure 8.2b feeds the 579 keV transition, which feeds the 1090 keV transition, which ﬁnally feeds the 152 keV transition to the ground state in 102Zr. This cascade of γ rays was also placed in the level scheme

**Original:** produced in this work. The main discrepancy between this work and previous work by Hill and Shizuma is the placement of the 1059 keV transition, as well as the inclusion of two additional transitions of 283-, and 764 keV.

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P114B007"></a><a id="P114B008"></a><a id="P114B009"></a><a id="P114B010"></a><a id="P114B011"></a><a id="P114B012"></a><a id="P114B013"></a><a id="P114B014"></a><a id="P114B015"></a><a id="P114B016"></a><a id="P114B017"></a><a id="P114B018"></a><a id="P114B019"></a><a id="P114B020"></a><a id="P114B021"></a>
<a id="S0223"></a>
**Source:** PDF114 S0223; native P114B007, P114B008, P114B009, P114B010, P114B011, P114B012, P114B013, P114B014, P114B015, P114B016, P114B017, P114B018, P114B019, P114B020, P114B021

**Original:** In this work, the gate on the 326 keV transition (shown in Figure 4.10) shows a coincidence with the 1059 keV transition. In order to observe this coincidence the 1059 keV transition must feed the 326 keV transition, or vice versa. However, in the level schemes produced by Hill and Shizuma [8] the 1059 keV transition directly feeds the 152 keV transition, by-passing the 326 keV transition and indicating no possibility for a coincidence between the 326 keV and 1059 keV transitions. There are two possible explanations for why a coincidence was not observed between the 326 keV and 1059 keV transitions in previous work. Fewer statistics were obtained in the experiments conducted by Hill and Shizuma, Figures 4.8 and 4.9 show a comparison between the 152 keV gate applied in this work, and the 152 keV gate published by Hill [8]. Due to the lack of statistics the 1059 keV transition may not have been observed in the previous work by Hill and Shizuma. Alternatively, there may be two 1059 keV transitions in 102Zr, one which decays to the 326 keV transition and another which decays to the 152 keV transition. Depending on the spin of the β decaying state 102Y is decaying from, the intensity of the two 1059 keV transitions may change considerably. In this work the states populated in 102Zr were produced in the

**中文:** 本工作326keV门谱（图4.10）中可见1059keV符合。因此1059keV必须位于326keV之前，或反过来。然而Hill与Shizuma[8]的能级纲图把1059keV直接放到152keV跃迁上方，绕过326keV，因而不可能产生1059–326符合。旧工作未见该符合有两种解释。首先是统计量较少，图4.8与4.9对比本工作和Hill的152keV门谱；1059keV符合可能因统计不足未见。另一种可能是102Zr有两条1059keV跃迁，一条馈入326keV跃迁，另一条馈入152keV跃迁。这两条线的强度可能随102Y β母态自旋显著变化。本工作中的102Zr态来自：

**中文图注:** 文献[8]发表的纲图：(a)102Y低自旋异能态的衰变；(b)102Y高自旋异能态的衰变。注意图中468keV标签有误，应为486keV。

## PDF 115 / 印刷p97

<a id="P115B002"></a><a id="P115B003"></a><a id="P115B004"></a><a id="P115B005"></a><a id="P115B006"></a><a id="P115B007"></a><a id="P115B008"></a><a id="P115B009"></a><a id="P115B010"></a><a id="P115B011"></a><a id="P115B012"></a><a id="P115B013"></a><a id="P115B014"></a><a id="P115B015"></a>
<a id="S0224"></a>
**Source:** PDF115 S0224; native P115B002, P115B003, P115B004, P115B005, P115B006, P115B007, P115B008, P115B009, P115B010, P115B011, P115B012, P115B013, P115B014, P115B015

**Original:** combined β decay of the low-spin and high-spin states in 102Y. Comparing the intensity ratios observed in this work with the ratios observed by Hill and Shizuma, 78% of the β decays observed originated from the high-spin state in 102Y. Therefore, it is possible that in the decay of the high-spin isomer of 102Y the intensity of the 1059 keV transition, which feeds the 326 keV transition dominates, and a 1059 keV transition just feeding the 152 keV transition is masked. In the previous work by Hill and Shizuma the level scheme produced for the decay of the high-spin isomeric state in 102Y, shown in Figure 8.2b, was deduced by combining the two studies at TRISTAN and JOSEF, the details are provided in Ref. [8]. In the level scheme a coincidence between the 1059 keV and 326 keV for the decay of the high-spin isomeric state in 102Y may not have been observed due to low statistics. Further work is required to measure the independent decay of the high-spin and low-spin states of 102Y and provide a conclusive answer to this problem. However, in this work due to the observed coincidence between the 1059-, and 326 keV transitions the 1059 keV transition was been placed feeding the 478 keV level.

**中文:** 102Y高、低自旋态的共同β衰变。与Hill和Shizuma强度比比较，所观察β衰变中78%来自102Y高自旋态。因此，高自旋异能态衰变中馈入326keV的1059keV线可能占优势，掩盖只馈入152keV的另一条1059keV线。旧高自旋能级纲图（图8.2b）由TRISTAN与JOSEF两研究合成，详见文献[8]；其中1059–326符合可能因低统计未见。仍需独立测量两态衰变才能作明确判断。本工作由于看到了1059–326符合，把1059keV跃迁置于478keV能级上方。

<a id="P115B016"></a><a id="P115B017"></a><a id="P115B018"></a><a id="P115B019"></a><a id="P115B020"></a><a id="P115B021"></a><a id="P115B022"></a><a id="P115B023"></a><a id="P115B024"></a><a id="P115B025"></a><a id="P115B026"></a><a id="P115B027"></a><a id="P115B028"></a><a id="P115B029"></a><a id="P115B030"></a><a id="P115B031"></a><a id="P115B032"></a>
<a id="S0225"></a>
**Source:** PDF115 S0225; native P115B016, P115B017, P115B018, P115B019, P115B020, P115B021, P115B022, P115B023, P115B024, P115B025, P115B026, P115B027, P115B028, P115B029, P115B030, P115B031, P115B032

**Original:** At IGISOL two additional transitions were observed, which were not observed at TRIS- TAN or JOSEF. A weak 283 keV γ ray was observed in coincidence with the 152-, and 326 keV transitions. However, in Figure 8.1 the 283 keV transition was positioned feeding the 1059 keV transitions, even though a coincidence with the 1059 keV transition was not observed in this work. One reason to support this placement is the energy diﬀerence of the 1821-, and 1538 keV energy levels, which is 283 keV matching the energy of the observed γ ray. Another reason to support the placement of the 283 keV transition is the relative intensity ratio of the 579-, and 283 keV transitions. In the singles spectra the ratio I283/I579 = 0.03, in the 160 keV gate the 579 keV peak was measured with a relative intensity of 75%, therefore, a relative intensity of 2% (∼2 counts) was expected for the 283 keV peak which is consistent with the measured spectrum. Therefore, although a coincidence between the 283 keV and 1059 keV γ rays was not observed, the 283 keV transition has been placed feeding the 1538 keV level. The energy of the 1059 keV γ ray is relatively high, a coincidence between the 283 keV γ ray and the 1059 keV γ ray may not have been observed due to the reduced detection eﬃciency at this energy. In this work the eﬃciency at 1059 keV was ∼3.6%, whereas the eﬃciency at 326 keV (a transition which was observed in coincidence with the 283 keV γ ray) was ∼7.6%.

**Original:** transitions, and was not seen in previous work at TRISTAN and JOSEF. Due to the observed coincidences with the 764 keV transition it has been placed feeding the 478 keV level. To support the placement of the 764 keV transition the relative intensity ratios of the 1090-, and 764 keV transitions were compared. In the singles spectra the ratio I764/I1090 = 11(3), in the 579 keV gate the 1090 keV peak was measured with a relative intensity of 34%, therefore, a relative intensity of 3% (∼4 counts) was expected for the 764 keV peak which is consistent with the measured spectrum, shown previously in Figure 4.12.

**中文:** 这些跃迁此前在TRISTAN和JOSEF未见。根据764keV的符合关系，将其置于478keV能级上方。为检验放置，比较1090与764keV相对强度。单谱中I764/I1090=11(3)，579keV门中1090keV峰相对强度为34%，因而预期764keV峰约为3%（约4计数），与图4.12所示测量谱相容。

<a id="P116B009"></a><a id="P116B010"></a><a id="P116B011"></a><a id="P116B012"></a><a id="P116B013"></a><a id="P116B014"></a><a id="P116B015"></a><a id="P116B016"></a>

**Original:** The placement of the 283-, and 764 keV gamma ray transitions in the level scheme is also conﬁrmed by a level scheme published by Durell, et al. [78]. At Daresbury laboratory an investigation into the spontaneous ﬁssion of a 248Cm source was performed and a partial level scheme for 102Zr produced, and is presented in Figure 8.3. In the level scheme published by Durell [78] the placement of both the 283-, and 764 keV transitions agree with the level scheme produced in this work, shown in Figure 8.1. Furthermore, in the level scheme published by Durell [78] the 1059 keV transition has also been positioned feeding the 326 keV transition, which also agrees with the level scheme produced in this

**中文:** Durell等[78]的能级纲图也支持283与764keV的放置。该研究在Daresbury研究248Cm源自发裂变，建立图8.3所示102Zr部分能级纲图。其283和764keV放置与本工作图8.1一致；1059keV也被置于馈入326keV的位置，与本工作相符。

<a id="S0229"></a>
**Source:** PDF118 S0229; native P118B003, P118B004, P118B005, P118B006, P118B007, P118B008, P118B009

**Original:** Supplementary to the experiments conducted at the IGISOL facility, data collected at the RIBF, at the RIKEN Laboratory, was also analysed, as detailed in Chapter 6. In this work the data was analysed under two criteria. Criteria 1 gated on the implantation of 102Sr and its subsequent decay into 102Y and 102Zr. Whereas criteria 2 gated on the implantation of 102Y and its subsequent decay into 102Zr. The aim of this work was to conﬁrm previous suggestions by Hill [8] and Shizuma [9] that there are two β decaying states in 102Y, which populate states in 102Zr with diﬀering intensity ratios.

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P118B010"></a><a id="P118B011"></a><a id="P118B012"></a><a id="P118B013"></a><a id="P118B014"></a><a id="P118B015"></a><a id="P118B016"></a>
<a id="S0230"></a>
**Source:** PDF118 S0230; native P118B010, P118B011, P118B012, P118B013, P118B014, P118B015, P118B016

**Original:** In the analysis of the RIKEN data γ ray intensities and γ-γ coincidences were measured. The γ ray intensities observed in this work showed that following implantation of 102Sr predominantly low-spin states were populated in 102Zr, supporting the results obtained at TRISTAN. However, when 102Y is implanted high-spin states were populated in 102Zr, which supports the results obtained at JOSEF. Analysis of the RIKEN data therefore conﬁrmed the assumptions previously made by Hill and Shizuma that states in 102Zr are populated with diﬀering intensity ratios depending on how 102Y is created before it decays.

**中文:** RIKEN分析测定了γ强度及γ–γ符合。102Sr植入后主要布居102Zr低自旋态，支持TRISTAN结果；102Y植入后则布居高自旋态，支持JOSEF结果。因此RIKEN分析确认了Hill和Shizuma此前的推测：102Y产生方式不同，会导致102Zr态的布居强度比不同。

<a id="P118B017"></a><a id="P118B018"></a><a id="P118B019"></a><a id="P118B020"></a><a id="P118B021"></a><a id="P118B022"></a><a id="P118B023"></a><a id="P118B024"></a><a id="P118B025"></a><a id="P118B026"></a>
<a id="S0231"></a>
**Source:** PDF118 S0231; native P118B017, P118B018, P118B019, P118B020, P118B021, P118B022, P118B023, P118B024, P118B025, P118B026

**Original:** Using the γ-γ coincidence information, presented in Table 6.2, a level scheme was produced for criteria 1 and 2, and compared with the level schemes published by Hill [8] and Shizuma [9]. The ﬁnal level schemes produced in this RIKEN analysis for criteria 1 and 2 are presented in Figures 8.4 and 8.5. The widths of the transition arrows are determined by the gamma-ray intensity of each transition, relative to the 152 keV transition, presented in Table 6.1. The spin and parity assignments for the two level schemes were taken partly from the previous work at TRISTAN [8] and JOSEF [8], and partly from the results of another experiment conducted by Durell, et al. at Daresbury Laboratory [78]. The level scheme published by Hill and Shizuma is presented in Figure 8.2, and the level scheme published by Durell is presented in Figure 8.3.

**中文:** 根据表6.2 γ–γ符合信息，分别建立条件1、2的能级纲图，并与Hill[8]、Shizuma[9]发表的纲图比较。最终纲图见图8.4、8.5，箭宽由表6.1各γ跃迁相对于152keV的强度确定。其中自旋宇称部分取自TRISTAN[8]及JOSEF[8]旧研究，部分取自Durell等在Daresbury的另一实验[78]。Hill与Shizuma纲图见图8.2，Durell纲图见图8.3。

## PDF 120 / 印刷p102

<a id="P120B002"></a><a id="P120B003"></a>
<a id="S0233"></a>
**Source:** PDF120 S0233; native P120B002, P120B003

**Original:** identiﬁed that was not seen in previous work at TRISTAN or JOSEF, and for criteria 2, the position of the 1059 keV transition populated in the decay of the high-spin isomeric

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P120B005"></a><a id="P120B006"></a><a id="P120B007"></a><a id="P120B008"></a><a id="P120B009"></a><a id="P120B010"></a><a id="P120B011"></a><a id="P120B012"></a><a id="P120B013"></a><a id="P120B014"></a><a id="P120B015"></a><a id="P120B016"></a><a id="P120B017"></a><a id="P120B018"></a><a id="P120B019"></a><a id="P120B020"></a><a id="P120B021"></a>
<a id="S0234"></a>
**Source:** PDF120 S0234; native P120B005, P120B006, P120B007, P120B008, P120B009, P120B010, P120B011, P120B012, P120B013, P120B014, P120B015, P120B016, P120B017, P120B018, P120B019, P120B020, P120B021

**Original:** For criteria 1, when gating on the 326 keV transition (shown in Figure 6.9a) the 1059 keV transition was observed to directly feed the 152 keV transition. This agrees with the level schemes published by Hill [8] and Shizuma [9]. However, for criteria 2, when gating on the 326 keV transition (shown in Figure 6.9b) the 1059 keV transition was observed to feed the 326 keV transition, which then feeds the 152 keV transition. This observation may have been due to a lack of statistics. In Figure 6.9b the 1059 keV peak was observed with 14% intensity relative to the 152 keV peak. Comparing this intensity to Figure 6.9a, if a coincidence with the 1059 keV transition was measured there should exist a peak at 1059 keV with an area of 3 counts. According to Figure 6.9a this is was not observed, however, more statistics are required to conﬁrm this conclusion. Alternatively, there may exist two 1059 keV transitions in the level scheme of 102Zr, as described in Section 8.1. A 1059 keV transition feeding the 326 keV transition was also observed in the IGISOL analysis. The IGISOL data was dominated by β decay from the high-spin state of 102Y. The RIKEN analysis also showed that following implantations of 102Y, which populated the high-spin states of 102Zr, a coincidence between the 1059-, and 326 keV transitions was observed. Previous work by Durell, detailed in Ref. [78], also supports the placement of the 1059 keV transition feeding the 326 keV transition.

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P120B022"></a><a id="P120B023"></a><a id="P120B024"></a><a id="P120B025"></a><a id="P120B026"></a><a id="P120B027"></a><a id="P120B028"></a><a id="P120B029"></a><a id="P120B030"></a><a id="P120B031"></a><a id="P120B032"></a><a id="P120B033"></a>
<a id="S0235"></a>
**Source:** PDF120 S0235; native P120B022, P120B023, P120B024, P120B025, P120B026, P120B027, P120B028, P120B029, P120B030, P120B031, P120B032, P120B033

**Original:** In the RIKEN analysis an additional transition at 764 keV was also identiﬁed, which was not observed in the previous work by Hill and Shizuma. For criteria 2, the 764 keV transition was been placed above the 152- and 326 keV transitions in the level scheme presented in Figure 8.5. To conﬁrm the placement of the 764 keV γ-ray transition the ratio of the relative intensity of the 764- and 1090- transitions in the singles spectrum, presented in Table 6.1 for criteria 2, was compared with the ratio of the 764- and 1090- transitions measured in the 579 keV coincidence spectrum for criteria 2. The ratio from the singles spectrum was I1090/I764=11(3), whereas the ratio from the coincidence spectrum was I1090/I764=7(3). The two obtained I1090/I764 ratios agree to within one standard deviation, supporting the placement of the 764 keV transition in this level scheme. This 764 keV transition, and its placement within the level scheme, is also supported by the IGISOL analysis and previous work by Durell, as shown in Figures 8.1 and 8.3.

**中文:** RIKEN分析还辨认出此前Hill与Shizuma未观察到的764keV跃迁。在条件2纲图8.5中，该线位于152及326keV跃迁上方。为检验放置，将表6.1条件2单谱中764与1090keV强度比，与条件2的579keV门谱中两线的强度比比较。单谱为I1090/I764=11(3)，门谱为7(3)；两者在一个标准差内相符，支持该放置。IGISOL分析和Durell旧研究也支持该线及其位置，见图8.1和8.3。

<a id="page-121"></a>
## PDF 121 / 印刷p103

<a id="P121B003"></a><a id="P121B004"></a><a id="P121B005"></a><a id="P121B006"></a><a id="P121B007"></a><a id="P121B008"></a>
<a id="S0236"></a>
**Source:** PDF121 S0236; native P121B003, P121B004, P121B005, P121B006, P121B007, P121B008

**Original:** As detailed in Chapter 7, BCS calculations were performed to provide a comparison with results obtained experimentally at RIKEN for the level scheme of 102Y. The BCS calculations, developed by Jain, et al. [68], were performed with the initial parameters ϵ2 = 0.3 [70], κ = 0.065 and µ = 0.32 for protons, and κ = 0.07 and µ = 0.39 for neutrons [71], and modiﬁcaions were made to the quasiparticle energies, as discussed in Section 7.2. The level scheme produced from these calculations is presented in Figure 8.6.

**中文:** 如第7章所述，本工作作了BCS计算，与RIKEN实验得到的102Y纲图比较。Jain等[68]开发的BCS程序采用初始ε2=0.3[70]、质子κ=0.065和μ=0.32、中子κ=0.07和μ=0.39[71]，并按7.2节修改准粒子能。所得纲图见图8.6。

<a id="P121B009"></a><a id="P121B010"></a><a id="P121B011"></a><a id="P121B012"></a><a id="P121B013"></a><a id="P121B014"></a><a id="P121B015"></a><a id="P121B016"></a><a id="P121B017"></a><a id="P121B018"></a><a id="P121B019"></a><a id="P121B020"></a><a id="P121B021"></a><a id="P121B022"></a><a id="P121B023"></a><a id="P121B024"></a><a id="P121B025"></a>
<a id="S0237"></a>
**Source:** PDF121 S0237; native P121B009, P121B010, P121B011, P121B012, P121B013, P121B014, P121B015, P121B016, P121B017, P121B018, P121B019, P121B020, P121B021, P121B022, P121B023, P121B024, P121B025

**Original:** The BCS calculations assigned 5−or 0−to the ground state of 102Y, from the coupling of a 5/2[532] neutron and a 5/2[422] proton. According to the Gallagher-Moszkowski coupling rules [76], detailed in Section 7.3.1, the 5−state has the lowest excitation energy. 5−was therefore assigned as the ground state spin and parity for 102Y. When performing BCS calculations, doublets, like the 5−and 0−states, are often considered to have an energy separation of a few hundred keV [26, p. 60]. In this calculation, an excitation energy of 300 keV was assigned to the 0−state, and as a result the 0−state can only decay to the 4+ or 5−states. This resulted in an expected γ decay half-life of the 0−state of 3.26 × 1012 seconds for the 0−state, according to the Weisskopf estimates [77, p. 69]. By comparison, the γ decay half-life of the 4+ state to the 5−ground state was calculated as 2.6 × 10−14 seconds, as was presented in Table 7.5. These half-lives were calculated from the transition probabilities given by the Weisskopf estimates listed in Table 7.6. A shorter half-life reﬂects a higher transition probability, therefore making γ ray transitions from the 0−state to the ground state in 102Y are much less probable than from the 4+ state to the ground state. As γ decay from the 0−state is so unlikely, β decay becomes a more likely decay path for this state. This makes the 0−state a strong candidate for the low-spin isomeric state in 102Y predicted by Hill and Shizuma [8].

**中文:** BCS将102Y基态候选定为5−或0−，来自5/2[532]中子与5/2[422]质子耦合。按7.3.1节Gallagher–Moszkowski规则[76]，5−能量最低，因此指定其为102Y基态自旋宇称。BCS计算中，此类双态常按几百keV分隔[26,p60]。本计算给0−指定300keV，因而它只能向4+或5−态退激。Weisskopf估计[77,p69]给出0−的γ半衰期3.26×10^12s，相比之下4+→5−的γ半衰期为2.6×10^−14s（表7.5）。这些值由表7.6的Weisskopf跃迁概率计算，较短半衰期意味着较高概率，因此0−到基态γ跃迁远不及4+到基态可能。由于0−的γ衰变如此不易，β衰变更可能，使其成为Hill和Shizuma[8]预言的102Y低自旋异能态的有力候选。

<a id="P121B001"></a>
**Source:** PDF121 P121B001; bbox [99.21, 46.25, 524.4, 57.16]

<a id="P123B002"></a><a id="P123B003"></a><a id="P123B004"></a><a id="P123B005"></a><a id="P123B006"></a><a id="P123B007"></a><a id="P123B008"></a><a id="P123B009"></a><a id="P123B010"></a><a id="P123B011"></a><a id="P123B012"></a><a id="P123B013"></a>
<a id="S0238"></a>
**Source:** PDF123 S0238; native P123B002, P123B003, P123B004, P123B005, P123B006, P123B007, P123B008, P123B009, P123B010, P123B011, P123B012, P123B013

**Original:** The level scheme in Figure 8.6 shows all the doublets calculated for 102Y. The Gallagher- Moszkowski coupling rules were applied to identify the lower energy states for each doublet. The higher energy state was given a default energy of ϵi+300 keV, where ϵi is the energy of the lower energy state of the doublet. A few hundred keV [26, p. 60] is a standard energy separation applied to the higher energy state of a doublet. However, in this case, if the 0−state is the low-spin isomeric state it cannot have an excitation energy of 300 keV, otherwise the two states in 102Y would not have been so diﬃcult to separate using the Ramsey cleaning technique at the IGISOL facility. The experiment performed at the IGISOL facility suggests that the energy diﬀerence between the ground and isomeric state in 102Y must be less than 100 keV. Combining the experimental data collected at IGISOL and RIKEN, as well as the results from the BCS calculations a ﬁnal level scheme for 102Y was produced, and is shown in Figure 8.7.

**中文:** 图8.6列出全部计算双态，并用Gallagher–Moszkowski规则确定每对较低者。较高者的默认能量取εi+300keV，εi为较低者能量。给双态较高者取几百keV间隔是常用做法[26,p60]。但这里如果0−是低自旋异能态，就不可能有300keV激发能，否则IGISOL的Ramsey清洗不应如此难以分离两态。IGISOL实验提示102Y基态与异能态的能差必须小于100keV。结合IGISOL、RIKEN数据与BCS结果，建立图8.7的最终102Y纲图。

<a id="P123B035"></a><a id="P123B036"></a><a id="P123B037"></a><a id="P123B038"></a><a id="P123B039"></a><a id="P123B040"></a><a id="P123B041"></a>
<a id="S0239"></a>
**Source:** PDF123 S0239; native P123B035, P123B036, P123B037, P123B038, P123B039, P123B040, P123B041

**Original:** The arrow widths in Figure 8.7 were determined from the intensity of each transition relative to the 244 keV transition, presented in Table 4.1. The spin and parity assignments for excited states feeding the 0−state in Figure 8.7 were obtained from the level scheme published by Hill in another experiment conducted at TRISTAN [37]. This second experiment by Hill also focused on studying the decay of 102Sr into 102Y. The level scheme published by Hill for 102Y is presented in Figure 8.8. The spin and parities for excited states in 102Y published by Hill are all low-spin (1+) states, as expected. According to

**中文:** 图8.7箭宽根据各跃迁相对244keV的强度确定，原文指向表4.1。图中馈入0−态的各激发态自旋宇称，取自Hill在TRISTAN另一实验[37]发表的纲图。该实验同样研究102Sr→102Y。Hill的102Y纲图见图8.8，其中激发态自旋宇称均为低自旋1+，符合预期。根据：

**Source:** PDF124 S0240; native P124B002, P124B003, P124B004, P124B005, P124B006, P124B007, P124B008, P124B009, P124B010, P124B011, P124B012, P124B013, P124B014, P124B015, P124B016, P124B017, P124B018, P124B019, P124B020, P124B021, P124B022, P124B023, P124B024, P124B025, P124B026, P124B027

**Original:** the electromagnetic transition rules, Equation 7.14, each of the 1+ states can decay to the 0−isomeric state via an E1 transition, which has a high transition probability. The level scheme produced in this work includes three transitions to the 0−state, at 94-, 209-, and 244 keV, whereas at TRISTAN six transitions were observed feeding the β-decaying state, at 94-, 209-, 244-, 312-, 498-, and 1689 keV. The 312-, 498-, and 1689 keV transitions were observed at TRISTAN with a relative intensity of 2.7-, 1.6-, and 3.0%, respectively, and were not seen in this work due to low statistics. In this work there were no transitions observed feeding the 209 keV transition, however, at TRISTAN three transitions at 36-, 103-, and 437 keV were observed feeding the 209 keV transition with a relative intensity of 1.0-, 1.8-, and 1.6%, respectively. These transition were not observed feeding the 209 keV transition in this work due to low statistics. In this work four transitions were observed directly feeding the 94 keV transition, at 115-, 150-, 217-, and 805 keV. This agrees with the coincidences observed at TRISTAN. However, at TRISTAN an additional 404 keV transition was observed feeding the 94 keV transition with a relative intensity of 1.1%, which was not observed in this work due to low statistics. In this work three transitions were observed directly feeding the 244 keV transition, at 68-, 254-, and 1105 keV. This agrees with the transitions observed at TRISTAN feeding the 244 keV transition. However, at TRISTAN a further two transitions were found in coincidence with the 244 keV transition at 655-, and 1446 keV with an intensity of 3.6-, and 6.1%, respectively, which were not observed in this work due to low statistics. In this work two transitions at 186-, and 1037 keV were observed feeding the 68 keV transition, which agrees with the coincidences observed at TRISTAN. However, at TRISTAN an additional 1378 keV transition was observed feeding the 68 keV transition with an intensity of 6.5%, which was not observed in this work due to low statistics. Finally, a 1193 keV transition was observed feeding the 186-, and 254 keV transitions, which agrees with the coincidences observed at TRISTAN. However, at TRIS- TAN an additional 850 keV transition was also observed feeding the 186-, and 254 keV

**Original:** are presented in Equation 8.1 [20, p.289]. According to Equation 8.1a allowed transitions occur between states with the same parity and a maximum change in spin of one unit. First forbidden transitions occur between states with diﬀerent parities and a maximum change in spin of two units, as shown in Equation 8.1b. Second forbidden transitions, shown in Equation 8.1c, occur between states with the same parity and a change in spin of two or three units only. When observing the decay path from 102Sr to 102Zr low-spin states are populated in 102Zr, which agrees with the β selection rules for allowed transitions. The order of forbiddenness of these decays is determined by the spin and parity of the state 102Y decays from. Although the spin and parity for the high-spin state in 102Y is not known, considering the β selection rules, allowed β decay from this high-spin state will

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P126B016"></a><a id="P126B017"></a><a id="P126B018"></a><a id="P126B019"></a><a id="P126B020"></a><a id="P126B021"></a><a id="P126B022"></a><a id="P126B023"></a>

**Original:** Predicting the β decay half-life is more complex. However, according to the β decay selection rules presented in Equation 8.1, and considering the levels populated in 102Zr, as shown in the level schemes in Figures 8.4 and 8.5, the two isomeric states in 102Y decay via a combination of allowed and ﬁrst forbidden β decay. Assuming the assignments 0− and 5−for the low-, and high-spin isomers in 102Y are correct. The less forbidden a β decay transition, the shorter the β decay half-life. The half-lives published for the low-, and high-spin isomers are 300 ms and 360 ms, respectively, as these decays are likely a combination of allowed and ﬁrst forbidden β decays.

**中文:** 预测β半衰期更复杂。不过按式8.1的选择规则及图8.4、8.5中102Zr被布居的能级，102Y两个异能态应通过允许和一级禁戒β衰变的组合退激，此处假定低、高自旋态分别为0−和5−的指认正确。β衰变禁戒程度越低，半衰期越短。文献给出的低、高自旋异能态半衰期分别为300ms与360ms，因为这些衰变很可能包含允许与一级禁戒分量。

**Original:** isomeric states in 102Y were produced with a 78:22 ratio, which is in strong agreement with the ratio 79:21 observed in previous work at JOSEF [8]. The RIKEN analysis showed that diﬀerent intensity ratios were measured for the γ transitions in 102Zr for the two decay paths investigated, 102Sr →102Zr and 102Y →102Zr. The level schemes established from the performed experiments conﬁrm the existence of two β-decaying states in 102Y. In this work new γ-ray transitions have also been identiﬁed in 102Zr.

<a id="P127B008"></a><a id="P127B009"></a><a id="P127B010"></a><a id="P127B011"></a><a id="P127B012"></a><a id="P127B013"></a><a id="P127B014"></a><a id="P127B015"></a><a id="P127B016"></a>

**Original:** Further to conﬁrming the previous claim made my Hill and Shizuma, the experimental data collected at RIKEN and IGISOL has expanded the level schemes published for 102Zr and 102Y. The level scheme for 102Zr now includes a 283- and a 764 keV transition. These two transitions were observed in the RIKEN analysis, following the implantation and subsequent decay of 102Y which populated high-spin states in 102Zr. It is therefore concluded that the 283- and a 764 keV transitions are observed with a higher intensity following the decay of the high-spin isomer in 102Y. In addition, the results obtained in this work have suggested there may exist two 1059 keV γ ray transitions, which depopulate diﬀerent states in 102Zr. The relative intensity of these 1059 keV transitions depends on which state 102Y

**中文:** 【待译；保留原文，本块未完成中文翻译。】

<a id="P127B018"></a><a id="P127B019"></a><a id="P127B020"></a><a id="P127B021"></a><a id="P127B022"></a><a id="P127B023"></a><a id="P127B024"></a><a id="P127B025"></a>
<a id="S0249"></a>
**Source:** PDF127 S0249; native P127B018, P127B019, P127B020, P127B021, P127B022, P127B023, P127B024, P127B025

**Original:** In the previous work by Hill and Shizuma it was not possible to provide spin or parity assignments for the long-lived states in 102Y. In this work, with the aid of BCS calculations, the ground and isomeric states in 102Y have been assigned a spin and parity value for the ﬁrst time as 5−and 0−respectively. These spin and parity assignments arise from the coupling of a 5/2[532] neutron and 5/2[422] proton. Combining these results with the experimental data collected, it can be seen that the 5−ground state predominantly populates negative parity states via allowed transitions, while the 0−isomeric states populates primarily positive parity states via ﬁrst-forbidden decays.

**中文:** 此前Hill和Shizuma无法给102Y长寿命态指定自旋宇称。本工作借助BCS首次把基态和异能态分别指定为5−及0−，来自5/2[532]中子与5/2[422]质子耦合。与实验数据结合，可见5−基态主要通过允许跃迁布居负宇称态，而0−异能态主要通过一级禁戒衰变布居正宇称态。

<a id="P127B026"></a><a id="P127B027"></a><a id="P127B028"></a><a id="P127B029"></a><a id="P127B030"></a><a id="P127B031"></a><a id="P127B032"></a>
<a id="S0250"></a>
**Source:** PDF127 S0250; native P127B026, P127B027, P127B028, P127B029, P127B030, P127B031, P127B032

**Original:** The experimental data collected at RIKEN also provided the opportunity to identify γ rays in 102Y and compare with a previous level scheme published by Hill for 102Y [37]. In the decay path 102Sr →102Y →102Zr γ rays from low-spin states, likely feeding the 0− isomeric state in 102Y were identiﬁed. The level scheme produced in this work agrees with the level scheme for 102Y published previously by Hill. As the low- and high-spin isomeric states in 102Y were not separated using the Ramsey cleaning technique at IGISOL an upper limit on the excitation energy of the low-spin 0−state of 100 keV has been assigned.

**中文:** RIKEN数据也可辨认102Y γ射线，并与Hill此前发表的102Y纲图[37]比较。102Sr→102Y→102Zr路径中，辨认出可能馈入102Y 0−异能态的低自旋态γ射线。本工作纲图与Hill纲图一致。由于IGISOL Ramsey清洗未分开高、低自旋异能态，本工作给低自旋0−态激发能指定100keV上限。

**Original:** By the early 1970s Cheifetz, et al. had identiﬁed the yrast bands in 102Zr [6], and Jared, et al., had determined the half-lives of the 2+ levels [79], and this was all that was known about the structure of 102Zr. To expand on this knowledge Shizuma, et al., conducted an experiment at the JOSEF recoil separator [9]. At the time of this experiment it was not possible to directly create 102Zr in conventional reaction experiments. Therefore, a study of the decay of 102Y was performed to obtain structural information for 102Zr. During this experiment γ singles and coincidences were measured and a level scheme for 102Zr was produced, which expanded on yrast states measured by Cheifetz.

**Original:** In 1991 a second experiment, also focusing on the structure of 102Zr was performed at the TRISTAN facility [8]. During this experiment, rather than separating 102Y using an ISOL system, 102Y was produced in the β decay of 102Sr. γ intensities measured at TRISTAN diﬀered considerably from those observed at JOSEF, as did γ coincidences. These observations are best explained if high-spin and low-spin β-decaying states were to exist in 102Y. It was suggested that at JOSEF, by observing the decay of 102Y to determine the structure of 102Zr, the high-spin isomer was produced in 102Y. By instead producing 102Y in the β decay of 102Sr to understand the structure of 102Zr the low-spin isomer in
